To address these challenges, we propose a data-centric training and deployment pipeline that integrates motion data curation, real-to-sim model adaptation, AMP-based reinforcement learning, and sim-to-real deployment. We validate the framework on the Booster T1 robot and further provide preliminary cross-platform validation on Booster K1.

</details>


### [146] [PPO-EAL: Exact Augmented Lagrangian Proximal Policy Optimization for Safe Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27861)
*Jiatao Ding,Songqun Gao,Andrea Del Prete,Matteo Saveriano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PPO-EAL框架，通过将精确增广拉格朗日优化集成到PPO中，在保持任务性能的同时实现精确的安全约束满足，在多个机器人基准测试中优于现有方法，并实现了零样本仿真到现实的部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习算法在精确满足约束的同时高效学习策略方面存在困难，需要一种既能保证安全性又不牺牲任务性能的实用方法。

Method: 提出PPO-EAL框架，将精确增广拉格朗日优化集成到近端策略优化中，结合裁剪策略更新与精确二次惩罚项，并使用动量调节的乘子更新提高对偶变量稳定性。

Result: 在车杆平衡、双摆稳定、Franka机械臂末端执行器到达、四足 locomotion 等GPU加速基准测试中表现出更优的安全精度和奖励性能，并在齿轮装配任务中实现了零样本仿真到现实部署，提高了任务成功率、降低了峰值接触力。

Conclusion: PPO-EAL是一种通用且可实际部署的安全强化学习框架，适用于多样化的安全关键型机器人系统。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising solution to accomplish complex robotic control tasks; however, most of the current work ignores the safety requirements. Safe RL seeks to maximize task performance while satisfying explicit physical constraints, but current algorithms struggle to learn the policy efficiently with precise constraint satisfaction. This work proposes PPO-EAL, a novel first-order constrained policy optimization framework that integrates exact augmented Lagrangian optimization into proximal policy optimization for safe robotic control. By combining clipped policy updates with exact quadratic penalty terms, PPO-EAL achieves theoretically grounded constraint enforcement without requiring impractically large penalty factors. A momentum-regulated multiplier update further improves dual-variable stability, reducing constraint oscillation and unsafe behavior while preserving task performance. We provide exactness and convergence analysis under standard stochastic approximation assumptions. Extensive validation across diverse GPU-accelerated robotic benchmarks-including cart-pole balancing, cart-double-pendulum stabilization, 7-DoF Franka end-effector reaching, and quadrupedal locomotion-demonstrates superior safety precision and reward performance compared with state-of-the-art first-order safe RL baselines. Finally, we demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real deployment in a contact-rich gear assembly task, where PPO-EAL substantially improves task success, reduces peak contact force, and enhances operational robustness. These results establish PPO-EAL as a general and practically deployable safe RL framework for diverse safety-critical robotic systems.

</details>


### [147] [LocalNav: Distilling Frontier VLMs and Embodied RL for On-Device Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27871)
*Nicolas Baumann,Liam Boyle,Pu Deng,Edoardo Ghignone,Boyang Sun,Marc Pollefeys,Luca Benini,Michele Magno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种蒸馏策略，将大模型的空间语义推理能力迁移到轻量级4B参数VLM上，并结合E-RLVR与Token生成正则化及量化技术，在边缘GPU设备上实现低延迟的物体导航任务，推理延迟降低82.8%，成功率仅从39.7%降至34.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在机器人领域具备强大环境感知能力，但其计算开销通常要求云执行，难以在资源受限机器人上实现低延迟本地部署。

Method: 1) 基于Claude Sonnet 4.6构建SotA场景图管线；2) 用500条前沿推理轨迹微调Qwen3.5-4B；3) 提出E-RLVR+Token生成正则化压缩输出序列长度；4) 结合量化进一步降低推理延迟。

Result: 蒸馏模型SR达34.5%（大模型39.7%）；E-RLVR使TG开销降低72.1%，延迟降低71.8%；联合量化实现累计82.8%延迟降低，性能无明显牺牲。

Conclusion: 该策略为移动机器人上本地、低延迟的VLM执行提供了可行范式，在性能与效率间取得有效平衡。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have emerged in the robotic domain as a powerful tool that enables environmental perception with language context, serving as a catalyst for open-vocabulary tasks like ObjectNav. Yet, their computational footprint typically confines them to cloud execution, hindering low-latency inference with local deployment on resource-constrained robots. To address this challenge, we present a distillation strategy that transfers complex spatial-semantic reasoning from large frontier models into a lightweight, 4B-parameter local VLM for edge execution on embedded GPU devices (e.g., Jetson Orin). We first establish a State of the Art (SotA), Scene Graph (SG)-based pipeline using Claude Sonnet 4.6, achieving a 39.7% Success Rate (SR) on the HM3D OVON benchmark. We then demonstrate that fine-tuning Qwen3.5-4B on just 500 frontier reasoning traces effectively enables navigation capabilities, yielding a SR of 34.5%, narrowing the gap to the performance of large cloud models. Finally, we introduce E-RLVR with Token Generation (TG) regularization to compress output sequence lengths for physical deployment while grounding the agent in its task. This downstream optimization reduces TG overhead by 72.1% and latency by 71.8%. Combined with quantization, this joint strategy yields a cumulative 82.8% reduction in overall inference latency without significantly sacrificing performance, presenting a viable paradigm for local, low-latency VLM execution on mobile robots.

</details>


### [148] [S$^2$-VLA: State-Space Guided Vision-Language-Action Models for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27872)
*Zhipeng Xie,Zongyi Han,Xiangyi Wei,Shiliang Sun,Yang Li,Jing Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出S²-VLA框架，通过状态空间引导的自适应注意力机制（SSGAA），在长期机器人操作任务中动态融合视觉、语言和动作特征，以2B参数超越7B模型，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在长期任务中性能严重下降，原因是采用静态特征融合机制，用固定权重组合视觉、语言和动作表征，无法适应任务执行的不同阶段。

Method: 提出SSGAA机制，维护一个跟踪任务进度的信念状态（belief state），生成动态门控权重，自适应融合视觉特征（空间感知）、任务意图（高层任务规划）和时序动作序列（执行一致性）三类信息。

Result: 仅2B参数规模，一致超越7B大模型，在LIBERO和SimplerEnv等长期操作基准上取得SOTA结果。

Conclusion: 自适应特征融合对长期机器人操作任务至关重要，为VLA模型处理复杂长程任务提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated strong capabilities in robotic manipulation, but their performance degrades significantly in long-horizon tasks due to cumulative error propagation. This limitation largely arises from static feature fusion mechanisms that rely on fixed weights to combine visual, language, and action representations, preventing the model from adapting to different phases of task execution. To address this limitation, we propose S$^2$-VLA, a framework that introduces a State-Space Guided Adaptive Attention (SSGAA) mechanism. SSGAA maintains a belief state that tracks task progression and generates dynamic gating weights to adaptively fuse information from three complementary sources visual features for spatial perception, task intents for high-level task planning, and temporal action sequences for execution consistency. This adaptive fusion allows the model to shift its focus throughout task execution, aligning with the evolving requirements of different task stages. Despite its compact 2B parameter size, S$^2$-VLA consistently outperforms larger 7B-scale models and achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-horizon manipulation benchmarks, including LIBERO and SimplerEnv. highlighting the importance of adaptive feature fusion for long-horizon robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [149] [Swarm sign language: motion-based communication between drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27883)
*Thomas Rey,Julien Moras,Alexandre Eudes,Antoine Manzanera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种面向隐身穿梭群机器人的运动基视觉通信方法，利用模块化平面轨迹作为视觉线索，通过3DTrajDecoder对时空序列进行分类、分割并回归尺寸和法向量，经在线程序化生成管道训练，在真实和仿真环境中验证了系统有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在群机器人系统中，主动无线电传输易被干扰或暴露位置，因此需要一种隐蔽的、非主动的信息交换方式。视觉通信作为替代方案，可在保持隐身约束的同时实现机器人间的信息传递。

Method: 利用模块化、动态可行的平面轨迹作为视觉线索；接收端无人机通过位姿估计器追踪发送端姿态，输入自定义的3DTrajDecoder；该解码器对时空序列进行分类和分割，同时回归其尺寸和法向量；通过可配置的在线程序化生成管道，同时使用通信和非通信轨迹对解码器进行鲁棒训练。

Result: 通过真实世界测试和仿真实验验证了系统的有效性，并进行了广泛的消融研究，详细说明了架构选择及系统局限性。

Conclusion: 运动基视觉通信方法在隐身约束的群机器人场景中具有可行性，为未来的非主动信息交互提供了有效途径，但系统仍存在一定局限性待进一步改进。

Abstract: In stealth-constrained swarm robotics, visual communication provides a critical alternative to active radio transmissions, which might be jammed. This research investigates motion-based communication for non-active information exchange, utilizing modular, dynamically feasible planar trajectories as visual cues. On the receiver drone end, a pose estimator tracks the transmitting drone's pose, feeding it into our custom 3DTrajDecoder. The decoder is designed to classify and segment the spatiotemporal sequence while simultaneously regressing its size and normal vector. To robustly train the decoder on both communicative and non-communicative trajectories, we developed a configurable online procedural generation pipeline. We validate our system through real-world testing and simulation to define its operating domain, supported by an extensive ablation study detailing our architectural choices and system limitations.

</details>


### [150] [Drifting in the Future: Stabilizing Path Following Drifting on High-Latency Vehicle Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27914)
*Frederik Werner,Till Heintzenberg,Markus Lienkamp,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于量产车的自主漂移控制框架，通过延迟补偿预测器、改进的控制公式和基于刹车的速度稳定策略，成功在配备内燃机和机械耦合车桥的量产跑车上实现了稳定漂移，横向误差控制在1.1米以内，侧滑角超调量低于0.06弧度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的自主漂移研究仅限于具备瞬时扭矩输出和独立车轮驱动的实验平台，其方法在存在执行器延迟和机械耦合车桥的量产车上是否可行尚不确定。本文旨在解决这一问题，将自主漂移能力推广至量产车型。

Method: 1) 设计预测器补偿动力总成延迟；2) 开发改进的控制公式以适应更高执行延迟及驱动桥差动耦合；3) 引入基于刹车的速度稳定策略。

Result: 控制器使量产跑车在超过250毫秒的执行器延迟下仍能稳健维持圆形和八字漂移，横向误差限制在1.1米，侧滑角超调量低于0.06弧度，同时减少振荡并保持稳定的路径和侧滑角跟踪。

Conclusion: 自主漂移在量产车上是可行的，为传统控制失效场景下能够稳定车辆的先进安全系统开辟了道路。

Abstract: Autonomously controlling and handling a vehicle at and beyond its stability limit is a mathematically and computationally demanding task. Prior demonstrations of automated drifting have been limited to research platforms with instantaneous torque delivery and independently actuated wheels, leaving their applicability to production vehicles with actuator latencies and mechanically coupled axles uncertain. To overcome these issues, we design a predictor to compensate for powertrain delays, develop a revised control formulation to accommodate higher actuation latencies as well as a differential coupling on the driven axle, and introduce brake-based velocity stabilization. This paper presents the controller framework, the model extensions, and real-world experimental results. We observe that our controller enables a production sports car with a combustion engine to robustly sustain circular and figure-eight drifts, limiting lateral error to 1.1 m and sideslip overshoot to 0.06 rad despite actuator delays exceeding 250 ms, while mitigating oscillations and maintaining stable path and sideslip tracking. In conclusion, our results establish that autonomous drifting is feasible on production-ready vehicles, opening pathways to advanced safety systems capable of stabilizing cars in scenarios where traditional control fails.

</details>


### [151] [When Multi-Robot Systems Meet Agentic AI:Towards Embodied Collective Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27929)
*Yuxuan Yan,Yuanyuan Jia,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了具身集体智能（ECI）概念，探讨多机器人系统如何超越传统的地图、任务和数据集共享，转向共享由具身智能体环路产生的状态（如世界上下文、任务进度和技能经验），并通过共感知、共行动、共演化三个维度构建共享资源框架。导航实验初步验证了共享世界记忆继承对新加入机器人的正向作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着具身AI向智能体化方向发展，以及机器人研究从单机自主走向多机器人系统，现有机器人团队仅共享地图、任务分配和数据集的模式已不足以支撑多智能体协作。需要一种新的范式，使机器人团队能够共享具身智能体环路产生的状态信息（世界上下文、任务进度、技能经验），从而提升多机器人系统的感知、决策和执行能力。

Method: 本文首先综述具身AI向智能体化演进的过程以及多机器人协作的发展历程；然后提出具身集体智能（ECI）概念框架，包含三个核心维度：共感知、共行动、共演化；最后通过一个导航场景的案例研究，对共享世界记忆继承这一具体组件进行验证性实验。

Result: 案例研究表明，新加入的机器人能够从合并的团队记忆中受益，验证了共享世界记忆继承的可行性。但研究明确指出该实验并非对ECI框架的完整评估，仅作为概念中一个可度量部分的初步验证。

Conclusion: 具身集体智能（ECI）是一个有前景的多机器人范式方向，为具身多智能体智能提供了概念框架和初步实验支撑，未来需要在共感知、共行动、共演化三个维度上进行更系统的研究和验证。

Abstract: Embodied AI is increasingly becoming agentic, shifting robots from perception--control pipelines towards closed-loop systems that can retrieve context, deliberate during execution, monitor feedback, and refine future behavior. In parallel, robotics research has also moved from single-robot autonomy towards multi-robot systems, driven by the need for wider sensing, distributed action, heterogeneous capabilities, and fault tolerance. As AI agents move from single-agent use towards multi-agent collaboration, robotics faces a parallel challenge: robot teams must move beyond sharing maps, task assignments, and datasets towards sharing the state produced by embodied agent loops. This article explores Embodied Collective Intelligence (ECI), a future multi-robot paradigm in which a robot team accumulates and uses world context, task progress, and skill experience as shared resources. Specifically, we first review how embodied AI is becoming agentic and how multi-robot cooperation has evolved. We then present Embodied Collective Intelligence through Co-Perception, Co-Action, and Co-Evolution. Finally, we use an illustrative navigation study to examine one concrete component of the concept: shared world-memory inheritance. The study shows that a newly added robot can benefit from merged team memory, but it is not intended as a full evaluation of the ECI framework. Taken together, the review and conceptual framework motivate Embodied Collective Intelligence as a direction for embodied multi-agent intelligence, while the case study grounds one measurable part of the concept.

</details>


### [152] [Building a Scalable, Reproducible, Evaluatable, and Closed-Loop Simulation Environment Foundation for Embodied Intelligence Cloud-Native Simulation Infrastructure for Embodied Intelligence Training, Evaluation, and Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27962)
*Junwu Xiong,Yongjian Guo,Mingxi Luo,Ning Qiao,Lei Kang,Song Wang,Yince Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向具身智能的云原生仿真基础设施框架，支持大规模训练、标准化评估和仿真数据收集，通过四层架构和云原生技术实现可扩展、可复现的仿真平台，并集成了多个代表性系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界机器人数据收集存在成本高、可扩展性有限、可复现性差等问题，亟需高效的大规模仿真基础设施来支撑具身智能的模型训练与评估。

Method: 采用云原生技术（弹性资源调度、容器化仿真、统一数据管理、服务化系统设计），构建四层架构（环境资产、任务生成、轨迹收集、评估优化），集成D-VLA、RL-VLA3、Sword、Pre-VLA等系统。

Result: 实现了支持多模型多任务负载的高效大规模仿真，具备动态调度、视觉增强、实时数据过滤等能力，形成数据生成-模型训练-标准化评估-真实部署的闭环。

Conclusion: 云原生仿真基础设施为具身智能的数据生成、模型训练、标准化评估和真实部署提供了统一基础，将在具身智能未来发展中发挥关键作用。

Abstract: This paper presents a cloud-native simulation infrastructure framework for embodied intelligence that supports large-scale training, standardized evaluation, and simulation-based data collection. The framework unifies simulation environment generation, task execution, trajectory collection, model evaluation, data management, and cloud services into a scalable and reproducible platform.
  To address the high cost, limited scalability, and poor reproducibility of real-world robotic data collection, the framework adopts cloud-native technologies including elastic resource scheduling, containerized simulation, unified data management, and service-oriented system design, enabling efficient large-scale simulation for multi-model and multi-task workloads.
  Built on a four-layer architecture, the framework provides standardized environment assets, automated task generation, trajectory collection, benchmark evaluation, and closed-loop data optimization. It further integrates representative systems including D-VLA, RL-VLA3, Sword, and Pre-VLA to support scalable simulation, dynamic scheduling, visual augmentation, and real-time data filtering.
  We argue that cloud-native simulation infrastructure provides a unified foundation for data generation, model training, standardized evaluation, and real-world deployment, and will play a key role in the future development of embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [153] [Translation as a Bridging Action: Transferring Manipulation Skills from Humans to Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28133)
*Sijin Chen,Kaixuan Jiang,Haixin Shi,Yanhui Wang,Weiheng Zhong,Haosheng Li,Bo Jiang,Yuxiao Liu,Xihui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种桥接动作表示（相对手腕平移），用于将人类操作技能有效迁移到双手机器人，相比传统的6DoF动作估计更具可扩展性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类动作数据廉价、丰富且多样，是扩展机器人学习的极佳资源，但将人类技能迁移到机器人面临两大挑战：人手姿态估计存在噪声，且人类手指与平行夹爪的接触模式根本不同，直接学习旋转相关的动作信号效果不佳。

Method: 提出了一种桥接动作表示：以初始头部相机坐标系为参照的相对手腕平移，这是人类和机器人共享的动作空间；并构建了一个类似π₀的视觉-语言-动作模型，采用交错动作标记和注意力掩码来处理不同本体中可能缺失的动作组件。

Result: 在一系列新型双人操作任务上，所提出的桥接动作比噪声6DoF人类动作更有效地将人类操作知识迁移到机器人，并且随人类数据量增加而扩展。

Conclusion: 相对手腕平移作为桥接动作表示是一种优于直接使用6DoF姿态的方法，能够实现从人类数据到双手机器人的更有效技能迁移，且具有数据可扩展性。

Abstract: We study whether we can learn novel manipulation skills from human actions to a bi-manual robot with parallel grippers. Human action data is cheap, abundant, and diverse, making it one of the most promising resources for scaling up robot learning. Yet transferring skills from humans to robots remains hard: most prior work treats humans as just another bi-manual 6DoF embodiment, where hand-pose estimates are noisy and the contact patterns of human fingers differ fundamentally from those of a parallel gripper. We argue that learning rotation-inclusive action signals from human data is therefore sub-optimal, and instead propose a bridging action representation: the relative wrist translation within the initial head-camera frame, an action space shared by humans and robots. To handle the potential absence of certain action components in different embodiments, we build a $π_0$-like vision-language-action model with interleaved action tokens and attention masking. On a suite of novel bi-manual manipulation tasks, our bridging action transfers human manipulation knowledge to robots far more effectively than noisy 6DoF human actions and scales with the amount of human data.

</details>


### [154] [PA-BiCoop: A Primary-Auxiliary Cooperative Framework for General Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28192)
*Bai Qicheng,Wang Ziru,Ma Teli,Dai Guang,Wang Jingdong,Wang Mengmeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PA-BiCoop，一种基于主-辅助臂动态分工的双臂协作框架，通过共享编码器和专用解码器实现双臂协调操作，在仿真和真实任务中分别比现有方法提升48%和50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双臂操作方法要么缺乏臂间交互，要么忽视动态分工需求，将双臂视为功能等同，限制了操作效率与灵活性。

Method: PA-BiCoop将机械臂分为主臂和辅助臂（角色可自适应调整），采用共享全局特征编码器+两个专用解码器：主解码器生成主臂基座坐标位姿和核心任务affordance热图，辅助解码器在主臂坐标系下输出相对位姿；同时设计动态角色分配模块自动映射左右臂角色。

Result: 在RLBench2仿真任务中平均超越SOTA基线48%，在真实世界任务中平均超越50%以上。

Conclusion: PA-BiCoop通过主-辅助臂动态分工机制有效提升了双臂协作性能，验证了其有效性与先进性。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation is essential for advanced robotic systems because it offers higher efficiency and flexibility compared to single-arm configurations. However, existing approaches either lack inter-arm interaction or ignore the need for a dynamic division of labor, treating the arms as functionally equivalent. To address these limitations, this paper draws inspiration from human bimanual manipulation where one arm handles core operations and the other provides auxiliary support, and proposes PA-BiCoop, a new single-model bimanual cooperation framework with dynamic primary-auxiliary arm differentiation. PA-BiCoop categorizes robotic arms into primary and auxiliary arms with adaptively adjustable roles across task stages, employs two specialized decoders that share a global feature encoder: the primary decoder generates the primary arm's base-coordinate pose and core-task affordance heatmaps, and the auxiliary decoder outputs the auxiliary arm's relative pose in the primary arm's coordinate system. Moreover, we design a dynamic role assignment module to automatically map roles to left/right arms without manual pre-definition. This design facilitates inter-arm knowledge sharing and coordinated manipulation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our PA-BiCoop achieves superior performance: it outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by 48% on average in RLBench2 simulation tasks and by over 50% on average in real world tasks, thereby verifying its effectiveness and advancement in bimanual manipulation.

</details>


### [155] [Learning Stable In-Grasp Manipulation in a Non-Dropping Action Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28196)
*Ha Thang Long Doan,Hikaru Arita,Kazuto Nakashima,Kenji Tahara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将灵巧操作技能分解为多个简单/可分析的组件，每个组件利用经典物理和控制理论中的先验知识进行约束和引导学习，从而高效探索稳定且精确的操作技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解析模型受限于强假设，而强化学习端到端方法因学习目标的不稳定和冲突导致效率低下，需要一种更高效的方法来探索稳定和精确的灵巧操作技能。

Method: 将灵巧技能分解为多个更简单/可分析的组件，每个技能组件在经典物理和控制理论的约束和引导下进行学习。

Result: 在稳定抓取、抓取内重定位/重定向等任务中，面对不同物体、传感器/电机噪声、延迟和摩擦条件，技能学习变得高效且稳定。

Conclusion: 将理论先验知识融入强化学习框架，可以有效提升灵巧操作技能学习的效率和稳定性。

Abstract: Traditionally, dexterous manipulation controllers are designed using analytic models constrained by strong assumptions about the hand and the objects being manipulated. Reinforcement learning (RL) has become another common approach in which skills are explored openly in an end-to-end manner but is inefficient because of unnoticeable instability and conflicts in learning objectives. This paper attempts to efficiently explore stable and accurate manipulation skills by decomposing dexterous skills into multiple simpler/analyzable components. Each skill component is subsequently learned with constraints and guidance from classical physics and control theory. Our work shows that for stable grasp, in-grasp reposition/reorientation with different objects, sensor/motor noise, latency, and frictional conditions, skill learning becomes efficient and stable with prior knowledge from theory.

</details>


### [156] [Unleashing Infinite Motion: Scaling Expressive Quadrupedal Motion via Generative Video Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28237)
*Youzhi Liu,Li Gao,Yifei Qian,Liu Liu,Yang Cai,Ziqiao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Uni-Mo全自动流水线，用LLM+视频扩散模型为四足机器人生成多样化运动数据，摆脱对动物动作捕捉的依赖，在真实机器人上实现96.7%部署成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人运动行为局限在少数步态，缺乏表达力；以往依赖动物动作捕捉存在数据采集困难、跨物种重建脆弱、跨形态重映射不适定等问题。

Method: Uni-Mo流水线：LLM提出运动提示→视频扩散模型合成机器人行为视频→Identity Consistency Loss保证帧间外观一致性→将视频提升为3D参考轨迹→训练跟踪策略部署到真实Unitree Go2机器人。

Result: 构建Quad-Imaginarium数据集（7,488个语言标注四足运动，18.5小时）；392个随机采样运动在真实Go2上96.7%部署成功率，完整数据集仿真97.6%成功率。

Conclusion: 移除动物环节、将数据稀缺重构为生成问题的范式是可行的，能够大规模生成高质量、多样化的四足机器人运动数据并成功部署到真实机器人。

Abstract: Quadruped robots have achieved remarkable locomotion, yet their behavioral repertoire remains confined to a few gaits--far from the expressive, companion-like presence long envisioned for them. Attempts to import the humanoid recipe of large-scale motion data have inherited one tacit assumption: that robot motion must first pass through an animal body, making data collection dependent on cooperative animals, reconstruction fragile across species, and retargeting ill-posed across incompatible morphologies. We propose Uni-Mo, a fully automated pipeline that removes the animal from the loop by reframing data scarcity as a generation problem: an LLM proposes motion prompts, a video diffusion model synthesizes the corresponding robot behaviors, and the generated videos are lifted into 3D reference trajectories used to train tracking policies deployed on a real Unitree Go2. To make naively-drifting generations reliably extractable, we introduce an Identity Consistency Loss that enforces appearance coherence across frames. We release Quad-Imaginarium at https://github.com/GaoLii/Quad-Imaginarium.git, the resulting open-source dataset of 7,488 language-annotated quadruped motions (18.5 hours) spanning acrobatic and performative behaviors. We validate 392 randomly sampled motions on a real Unitree Go2 with a 96.7% deployment success rate, complemented by a 97.6% success rate across the full dataset in simulation.

</details>


### [157] [SimFoundry: Modular and Automated Scene Generation for Policy Learning and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28276)
*Nadun Ranawaka,Josiah Wong,Wei-Lin Pai,Wei-Teng Chu,Tianyuan Dai,Masoud Moghani,Hang Yin,Yunfan Jiang,Wesley Durbano,Brandon Huynh,Yu Fang,Linxi Fan,Danfei Xu,Ruohan Zhang,Li Fei-Fei,Bowen Wen,Ajay Mandlekar,Yuke Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimFoundry是一个模块化自动化系统，能从视频零样本构建真实到仿真的场景，生成数字孪生和数字表亲（保持功能特性的场景变体），用于训练机器人策略，实现零样本迁移到真实世界任务。在7个操作任务和5种策略架构上，仿真评估与真实性能高度相关（Pearson相关系数均值0.911），使用物体、场景和任务变体训练分别提升17%、21%和40%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中训练和评估机器人策略成本高、难以规模化，需要一种高效、可扩展的仿真数据生成方法，实现从真实场景到仿真环境的自动化转换并支持策略的零样本迁移。

Method: SimFoundry采用模块化自动化管道，从视频零样本构建真实到仿真的场景，生成可编辑的数字孪生，并自动生成保持功能特性的数字表亲（场景、物体和任务的变体），用于在仿真中训练机器人策略。

Result: 在7个操作任务和5种策略架构上，仿真评估与真实性能高度相关（Pearson相关系数均值0.911，最大排名违规均值0.018）；使用物体、场景和任务数字表亲训练分别提升零样本真实世界成功率17%、21%和40%。

Conclusion: SimFoundry能够高效生成仿真数据，训练的策略可零样本迁移到复杂真实操作任务（包括多步操作、铰接物体交互和双臂操作），数字表亲机制有效增强了策略对新场景的泛化能力。

Abstract: Training and evaluating robot policies in the real world is costly and difficult to scale. We introduce SimFoundry, a modular and automated system for zero-shot real-to-sim scene construction from a video. SimFoundry generates sim-ready digital twins and supports object, scene, and task editing, enabling the automated generation of diverse digital cousins: affordance-preserving variations of reconstructed real-world scenes. Policies trained on SimFoundry data transfer zero-shot to challenging real tasks involving multi-step manipulation, articulated object interaction, and bimanual interaction, and its digital cousins (variations of the original scene, objects, and tasks) facilitate generalization to new real-world conditions. Across 7 manipulation tasks and 5 policy architectures, SimFoundry simulation evaluations strongly predict real-world performance, with mean Pearson correlation 0.911 and mean maximum ranking violation 0.018. When evaluating sim-trained policies zero-shot in the real world, policies trained with object, scene, and task cousins in simulation show average task success rate improvements of 17%, 21%, and 40%, respectively. Additional details at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/gear/simfoundry/ .

</details>


### [158] [CacheMPC: Certified Cached Model Predictive Control for Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28300)
*Nimesh Khandelwal,Mehul Anand,Shakti S. Gupta,Mangal Kothari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Certified CacheMPC，通过局部敏感哈希缓存接触力轨迹并结合后验认证（检查原始可行性和对偶间隙上界），在保证控制性能的同时将四足机器人MPC求解速度提升25倍（仿真）和18.7倍（实物），且不显著降低闭环稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MPC在分层四足控制器中每周期需求解QP问题，限制了嵌入式处理器上的更新率。由于步态运动反复访问有界状态空间，MPC解具有可缓存和复用的潜力，因此本文旨在利用这一特性加速在线求解。

Method: 提出Certified CacheMPC框架：1）使用局部敏感哈希（LSH）对按接触模态分区的horizon接触力轨迹进行缓存；2）查询时检索候选解，并通过后验认证（验证原始可行性和Lagrangian对偶间隙上界）决定是否接受；3）结合Top-K认证检索、截止时间有界QP求解和上一认证解回退的有界预算控制器调度策略。

Result: 在Unitree Go2上进行了2038次可用冷启动MuJoCo仿真试验（含600次n=50的3个失效边界单元测试）和首次实物部署（NVIDIA Orin NX）。无门控缓存实现仿真中25倍中位数加速，硬件上18.7倍。n=50时，缓存变体与无缓存基线在各测试单元的闭环稳定率无统计显著差异。证书对闭环安全的贡献在现有样本量下无法分辨。

Conclusion: Certified CacheMPC通过缓存和认证机制大幅提升四足机器人MPC求解效率，同时保持闭环稳定性；但后验认证对安全性的实际增益仍需更大规模实验验证。

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) is the standard predictive layer in hierarchical quadruped controllers, but the per-cycle QP solve limits the update rate achievable on embedded processors. Because legged gaits revisit a bounded region of state space, MPC solutions admit caching and reuse. This paper proposes \emph{Certified CacheMPC}: a Locality-Sensitive-Hashed cache of horizon contact-force trajectories, partitioned by contact mode, retrieved at query time and accepted only when an a-posteriori per-query certificate confirms primal feasibility and a Lagrangian dual-gap upper bound on cost suboptimality. A bounded-budget controller schedule combines top-$K$ certified retrieval, a deadline-bounded QP solve, and a shifted last-certified fallback. The framework is evaluated on a Unitree Go2 across $2{,}038$ usable cold-controller MuJoCo trials, including a $600$-trial $n\!=\!50$ campaign at three failure-boundary cells, and a first-deploy session on the on-robot NVIDIA Orin NX. The un-gated cache delivers a $25\times$ median solve-time speedup in simulation and an $18.7\times$ median speedup on hardware. At $n\!=\!50$ no statistically significant difference in closed-loop stable rate is detected between the cache variants and the no-cache baseline at any tested cell. The certificate's contribution to closed-loop safety is not resolvable at the present sample size.

</details>


### [159] [WARP-RM: A Warp-Augmented Relative Progress Reward Model for Data Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28320)
*Justin Yu,Andrew Goldberg,Kavish Kondap,Karim El-Refai,Ethan Ransing,Qianzhong Chen,Mac Schwager,Fred Shentu,Philipp Wu,Ken Goldberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WARP算法，通过时间扭曲数据增强自监督学习相对进度信号，用于改进行为克隆中次优演示数据的学习效率。在双臂机器人折T恤任务上，相比原始行为克隆，在含次优数据时成功率从10%提升至95%，吞吐量提升18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类遥操作产生的演示数据质量参差不齐（包含犹豫、恢复等），而现有的帧级进度奖励模型要么基于有噪声的绝对时间代理监督，要么需要昂贵的人工标注来定义子任务边界。

Method: WARP包含两个核心组件：1) WARP-RM：通过对演示进行时间扭曲增强（变速播放和倒放）生成自监督的帧间归一化经过时间预测目标，再跨重叠窗口聚合得到密集帧级进度信号；2) WARP-BC：利用这些标量奖励估计对高优势的动作块（chunk）进行上加权，其中块级优势通过聚合帧级奖励得到。

Result: 在双臂机器人折叠T恤（从随机揉皱状态开始）的长程变形物体操作任务上，当训练数据集包含更多次优数据时，WARP-BC保持19/20成功率，而原始行为克隆降至2/20，吞吐量提升高达18倍。

Conclusion: WARP算法能够完全自监督地从成功演示中学习稠密的、有符号的相对进度信息，有效提升对次优演示数据的鲁棒性，在长程机器人操作任务中显著优于原始行为克隆。

Abstract: Scaling imitation learning requires large datasets, yet human teleoperation inevitably produces mixed-quality demonstrations containing hesitations and recoveries. Prior frame-level progress reward models supervise on absolute temporal progress proxies that suffer from label noise, or require costly human annotations to define subtask boundaries. We present WARP (Warp-Augmented Relative Progress), a novel fully self-supervised algorithm for learning dense, signed relative progress magnitudes directly from successful demonstrations. WARP generates per-frame progress targets via time-warp augmentations of demonstrations (variable playback speeds and reversals) and we train WARP-RM to predict the normalized elapsed time between input frames. Aggregating these predictions across overlapping windows yields a dense frame-level progress signal. We then introduce WARP-BC, which leverages these scalar reward estimates to upweight high-advantage action chunks during behavior cloning, where chunk-level advantage is obtained by aggregating per-frame rewards. We evaluate our approach on a physical bimanual robot system performing a long-horizon deformable object manipulation task: folding T-shirts from a random crumpled start. To evaluate policy robustness against suboptimal data, we construct training datasets of varying quality using episode length as a proxy for teleoperation sub-optimality. As the dataset is widened to admit more inefficiencies, WARP-BC maintains a 19/20 success rate compared to vanilla BC's collapse to 2/20, improving throughput by up to 18x.

</details>


### [160] [DexCompose: Reusing Dexterous Policies for Multi-Task Manipulation with a Single Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28323)
*Dihong Huang,Zhenyu Wei,Zhuxiu Xu,Yunchao Yao,Sikai Li,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexCompose提出了一种基于手指级别动作所有权的残差组合框架，通过识别保留技能所需的手指并分配动作子空间，实现了灵巧操作技能的零冲突组合。在16个复合任务上平均成功率达77.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧操作策略可解决单一技能，但组合多个技能时，不同任务对同一手指和接触模式产生冲突需求，导致已有操作结果被破坏，缺乏有效的多任务组合方法。

Method: DexCompose包含三步：1) 从第一技能收集成功状态并执行释放测试，识别维持技能所需的手指掩码；2) 训练两个非对称残差模块——有界残差稳定器用于任务保持，上下文感知残差用于在新任务分配的动作子空间内适配冻结的下游策略。

Result: 在16个复合灵巧操作任务上平均复合成功率达77.4%，验证了结构化的动作所有权结合双重残差的有效性。

Conclusion: DexCompose证明了手指级显式动作所有权与双重残差机制是超越传统策略链式组合、实现灵巧技能组合的可行方向。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation policies can solve individual skills, but composing them to perform multiple tasks with a single hand remains challenging. Adding a new task on top of an existing manipulation skill often imposes conflicting demands on overlapping fingers and contact modes, causing destructive interference between preserving an existing manipulation outcome and executing a new one. We propose DexCompose, a role-aware residual composition framework that reuses pretrained dexterous policies for multi-task manipulation through explicit finger-level action ownership. Given two pretrained full-hand policies, DexCompose first collects successful post-task states from the first skill and performs release tests over candidate finger masks to identify which fingers are necessary for maintaining the established skill state. It then trains two asymmetric residual modules: a bounded residual stabilizer for task preservation, and a context-aware residual that adapts the frozen downstream policy only within the action subspace assigned to the new task. We evaluate the framework on 16 composite dexterous manipulation tasks spanning four object-retention skills and four downstream interactions. DexCompose achieves a 77.4% average composite success rate, demonstrating that structural action ownership with dual residuals offers a promising direction for composing dexterous skills beyond conventional policy chaining.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [HandMade: Spatial Prompting for Generative 3D Creation with Part-Labeled VR Sketches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27738)
*Jialin Huang,Rana Hanocka,Ariel Shamir,Yotam Gingold*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: HandMade是一种结合VR 3D草绘与语言提示的交互范式，通过将用户绘制的带部件标签的3D草图作为空间提示输入现有生成模型，实现比纯文本或纯草图基线更优的空间布局保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯文本在3D内容生成中无法有效表达空间意图（部件位置、相互关系及整体结构），需要一种更直观的3D空间指定方式。

Method: 将VR绘制的分段3D草图转换为多视图部件引导信号和结构化提示，其中3D草图指定空间布局，语言负责身份、材质、风格和局部细节。

Result: 在20个多样化样例上，HandMade比纯文本和基于草图的基线方法更好地保留了用户创作的空间骨架；8人用户研究验证了用户对3D草绘和语言分工使用的行为模式。

Conclusion: HandMade提供了一种面向空间引导的3D生成的交互范式与接口-生成管道，有效弥补了文本在空间指定上的不足。

Abstract: Text-to-3D generation lowers the barrier to 3D content creation, but text alone is a weak interface for specifying spatial intent: where parts should be placed, how they relate, and how an object should be organized in 3D. We present HandMade, a workflow that combines VR 3D sketching and language for open-domain 3D asset generation. HandMade treats coarse, part-labeled 3D sketches not as incomplete geometry to reconstruct directly, but as spatial prompts for existing generative models. It converts segmented VR strokes into multi-view part guidance and structured prompts, allowing users to specify object layout and part relationships through 3D sketching while using language for identity, material, style, and local details. A technical evaluation shows that HandMade better preserves user-authored spatial scaffolds than text-only and sketch-based baselines on 20 varied examples. A user study with eight participants characterizes how users make use of 3D sketching for spatial layout and language for identity, materials, and details across initial authoring and subsequent revision. HandMade contributes an interaction paradigm and interface-to-generation pipeline for spatially guided 3D creation.

</details>


### [162] [Context-Aware Explanations for Spatialized Document Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28081)
*Wei Liu,John Wenskovitch,Chris North,Rebecca Faust*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CAPE是一个上下文感知的解释框架，通过结合文档语义和布局空间上下文，生成自然语言解释来帮助用户理解文档布局中的空间组织关系（如聚类、子群、异常点和桥接文档），并支持AI引导概览和用户驱动探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要总结文档内容或解释布局生成方式，对理解布局内部空间关系的支持有限，用户难以解读文档在空间布局中的组织模式和区域间关系。

Method: CAPE识别显著空间模式（聚类、子群、异常点、桥接文档），构建多层次上下文表示，引导LLM生成基于空间上下文的自然语言解释，支持AI引导概览和用户驱动探索，并提供多种细节级别的解释。

Result: 在新闻和学术文档布局上进行了演示，并通过受控用户研究对比基于关键词和仅内容的LLM基线，结果表明空间上下文化解释比仅内容基线在帮助用户理解文档布局空间组织方面更有帮助。

Conclusion: 空间上下文的解释对于理解文档布局的空间组织更为有效，CAPE框架通过融合语义和空间信息提升了可解释性。

Abstract: Spatialized document layouts are widely used for exploratory analysis of text corpora, but interpreting the spatial organization of documents and the relationships between regions remains challenging. Existing approaches primarily summarize document content or explain how layouts are generated, providing limited support for understanding spatial relationships within the layout itself. We present CAPE, a context-aware explanation framework that generates natural-language explanations grounded in both document semantics and layout-derived spatial context. CAPE identifies salient spatial patterns (e.g., clusters, subgroups, outliers, and bridging documents) and constructs multi-level contextual representations to guide LLM-based explanation generation. It supports both AI-guided overview and user-driven exploration, with explanations available at multiple levels of detail. We demonstrate CAPE on news and scholarly document layouts and evaluate it in a controlled user study against keyword-based and content-only LLM baselines. Our results suggest that spatially grounded explanations are perceived as more helpful than content-only baselines for interpreting the spatial organization of document layouts.

</details>


### [163] [Typing Behavior in Human-LLM Interaction: Keystroke Dynamics Reveal Cognitive Effort During Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28090)
*Laura Schütz,Yousri Cherif,Clara Sayffaerth,Thomas Weber,Francesco Chiossi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了击键动态作为人类与大语言模型交互过程中用户心理努力和感知输出有用性的行为指标。通过36人用户实验发现，困难任务导致更多击键、更慢打字、更多停顿和更高主观工作负荷；设备类型影响较弱；击键能捕捉认知努力差异，但无法预测感知的LLM输出有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM融入日常生活，理解用户如何与这些系统交互对于有效的人机协作至关重要。本研究旨在探索击键动态能否作为衡量用户在LLM交互中认知努力和感知输出有用性的行为指标。

Method: 招募36名参与者进行用户实验，操作变量为任务难度（简单 vs. 困难）和设备类型（桌面端 vs. 移动端），测量击键行为指标（击键次数、打字速度、停顿等）以及主观工作负荷（NASA-TLX）。

Result: 困难任务导致显著更多的击键次数、更慢的打字速度、增加的停顿和更高的自报工作负荷。设备类型影响较弱，移动端使用略微减少输入长度和打字速度。击键指标能捕捉认知努力差异，但不能预测用户对LLM输出有用性的感知。

Conclusion: 击键动态有潜力作为LLM提示过程中认知努力的实时指标，但在捕捉用户感知的协作成功方面存在局限性。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) become increasingly integrated into daily routines, understanding how users interact with these systems is crucial for effective human-AI collaboration. This work investigates keystroke dynamics as a behavioral measure of user mental effort and perceived output usefulness in human-LLM interaction. We conducted a user study (N = 36) to examine how task difficulty (easy vs. hard) and device type (desktop vs. mobile) influence typing behavior and workload (NASA-TLX) during interactions. Our results indicate that hard tasks led to significantly more keystrokes, slower typing, increased pauses, and higher self-reported workload. Device type had weaker effects, with mobile use slightly reducing input length and typing speed. While keystrokes captured differences in cognitive effort, they did not predict perceived LLM output usefulness. These findings highlight the potential of keystroke dynamics as real-time indicators of cognitive effort during LLM prompting, while also showing their limitations in capturing perceived collaboration success.

</details>


### [164] [Functional outcomes and naturalistic engagement with a purpose-built conversational AI for mental health (Ash)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28241)
*Kristen M. Van Swearingen,Thomas D. Hull,Karthik V. Sarma,Caitlin A. Stamatis*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 一项针对1,284名真实用户的单臂观察性队列研究表明，使用心理健康对话AI助手Ash四周后，用户在生活满意度、关系满意度、睡眠质量、行为激活和工作联盟等心理功能指标上均有显著改善（效应量d=0.14-0.26），且未出现自我膨胀（夸大自我认知）等潜在危害。用户参与度（活跃天数、总会话次数、总使用时长）与第4周的心理功能和工作联盟显著相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注心理健康AI对临床症状的缓解效果，而缺乏对更广泛的日常心理功能指标（如生活满意度、睡眠质量等）的评估，同时也极少监测潜在危害（如自我感知膨胀）。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 单臂观察性队列研究，纳入1,284名新用户，在基线和第4周通过应用内单项目测量评估心理功能（生活满意度、关系满意度、睡眠质量、行为激活）、工作联盟和夸大自我认知。采用配对样本t检验分析个体内变化，ANCOVA检验参与度与结果的关联（控制基线水平）。

Result: 所有心理功能指标和工作联盟均出现显著个体内改善（p<.001，d=0.14-0.26），夸大自我认知无显著变化。活跃天数、总会话次数和总使用时长一致预测第4周心理功能和工作联盟（p≤.006，偏R²范围0.58%-2.15%），而用户消息量则无此预测作用。

Conclusion: 初步证据表明，基于证据的对话式AI有潜力在临床症状之外，扩展对广泛心理功能的心理健康支持，且未观察到自我膨胀等短期危害。

Abstract: Background: Conversational AI chatbots designed for mental health may offer an accessible, scalable avenue for supporting psychological well-being, yet prior evaluations have largely focused on clinical symptom reduction rather than broader indicators of day-to-day functioning, and have rarely monitored for potential harms such as inflated self-perception.
  Objective: We examined within-person change in psychological functioning indicators among real-world users of Ash, a purpose-built conversational AI for mental health support, over the first four weeks of use, and whether these changes were associated with engagement metrics.
  Methods: In this single-arm observational cohort study, new users (n = 1,284) completed in-app single-item measures of psychological functioning (life satisfaction, relationship satisfaction, sleep quality, behavioral activation), working alliance, and grandiosity (inflated self-perception), at baseline and Week 4. Paired-sample t-tests examined within-person change; ANCOVAs tested engagement-outcome associations at Week 4, controlling for baseline.
  Results: At baseline, participants reported below-average life satisfaction and fair sleep quality. Significant within-person improvements emerged across all functioning indicators and working alliance (ps < .001; d = 0.14-0.26), with no change in grandiosity. Active days, total sessions, and total minutes consistently predicted Week 4 psychological functioning and working alliance (ps <= .006; partial R^2 range: 0.58-2.15%; controlling for baseline), whereas user message volume did not.
  Conclusion: Findings provide preliminary data for the potential of evidence-based conversational AI to extend mental health support for broad psychological functioning, extending the existing literature beyond symptom-based outcomes.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [AI-Model Network: Concept, Current State and Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27382)
*Li Zhetao,Zeng Xiyu,Wang Jianhui,Xiao Yong,Liu Zhongren,Wu Junru,Lai Junjie,Huang Jijun,Long Saiqin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文受互联网发展启发，提出世界人工智能模型网络（AI-ModelNet）的概念、愿景和系统架构，旨在通过建立模型间的通路实现异构大模型的互联互通、能力共享与协同推理，解决当前大模型应用中的高成本和部署复杂性难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大模型面临训练成本高、部署复杂的困境，且异构模型快速 proliferation 导致模型间缺乏有效的交互与协作机制，成为制约发展的关键瓶颈。

Method: 借鉴互联网的发展模式，提出AI-ModelNet范式，设计分层系统架构，并通过原型系统与多样化应用案例验证框架的可行性。

Result: 原型系统验证了所提框架的可行性，展示了模型间互联互通与协同推理的能力。

Conclusion: AI-ModelNet是一种有望突破当前单模型局限的新范式，未来研究方向包括协议标准化、安全性和可扩展性等关键问题。

Abstract: While the primary function of computers lies in computation and processing, the core value of the Internet is rooted in sharing and collaboration. Computers create the Internet, and the Internet empowers the value of computers. The rapid development of the Internet, cloud computing, and big data is pushing artificial intelligence into the era of large models (LMs). However, the practical application of LMs is currently hindered by high training costs and deployment complexities, driving a shift toward lightweight, private, and domain-specific models. With the rapid proliferation and wide distribution of heterogeneous models, enabling effective interaction and collaboration among them has emerged as a critical bottleneck that urgently needs to be addressed in LM development. Drawing inspiration from the development of the Internet, this paper proposes the concept, vision, and system architecture of world wide AI-model network (AI-ModelNet). It is a novel paradigm that achieves interconnection, capability sharing, and collaborative reasoning by establishing pathways between models. We first briefly review the current state of single-model and multi-model research. Subsequently, the systemic vision and hierarchical architecture of AI-ModelNet are articulated, followed by validation of the framework's feasibility through a prototype system and diverse application cases. Finally, key directions for future research are discussed preliminarily.

</details>


### [166] [DysLexLens: A Low-Resource LLM Framework for Analysing Dyslexic Learners Insights from Online Forums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27619)
*Dana Rezazadegan,Atie Kia,Phongpadid Nandavong,Dominique Carlon,Jeremy Nguyen,Abhik Banerjee,James Marshall,Anthony McCosker,Yong-Bin Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DysLexLens，一个低资源LLM框架，用于通过在线论坛讨论分析失读症学习者使用AI工具的体验。该框架包含词典驱动过滤、知识图谱推理、定量评估和定性验证等功能，在Reddit数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 失读症学习者越来越多地使用AI工具辅助学习，但他们对这些工具的实际体验尚未得到充分研究，缺乏系统分析框架来从低资源论坛数据中挖掘相关信息。

Method: DysLexLens是一个端到端、可追溯证据的架构，包含：(1)词典驱动过滤方法构建聚焦失读症与AI的Reddit语料库；(2)LLM辅助语义分析与知识图谱查询推理；(3)RAGAS和查询鲁棒性等定量评估指标；(4)结构化定性验证指南评估幻觉和证据对齐。

Result: 在失读症相关Reddit论坛数据和30个问题上验证了有效性，展示了其推广到其他低资源论坛数据环境的潜力。

Conclusion: DysLexLens为分析失读症学习者AI体验提供了低资源、可复现的LLM框架，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Dyslexic learners increasingly use artificial intelligence (AI) tools to support reading, writing, organisation, and study-related tasks. However, their lived experiences with these tools remain largely underexamined. This paper proposes DysLexLens, a low-resource LLM framework, designed to analyse dyslexic learners experience with AI through online forum discussions. DysLexLens is designed as an end-to-end, evidence-traceable architecture which transforms noisy social media posts into a dictionary-driven corpora, provides knowledge-graph (KG)-based question reasoning, generates verifiable query responses, and enables response evaluation through quantitative and human-grounded assessment. DysLexLens has four key features. First, it employs a dictionary-driven filtering method to construct a more focused Reddit corpus on dyslexia and AI, filtering out noisy and weakly related posts to improve the relevance of data collected from low-resource forum contexts. Second, it integrates LLM-assisted semantic analysis with KG-based query reasoning to uncover meaningful patterns. Third, it has quantitative evaluation metrics (RAGAS and Query Robustness) to measure LLM-generated response performance. Fourth, it provides structured qualitative validation guidelines for assessing response quality, with a specific focus on hallucination and evidence alignment. We demonstrate the effectiveness of DysLexLens using dyslexia-related Reddit forum data and 30 questions. The results show its potential generalisability to other low-resource forum data contexts. DysLexLens, sample data, questions and evaluation results are available at Github to support reproducibility.

</details>


### [167] [When Does Personality Composition Matter for Multi-Agent LLM Teams?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27443)
*Aryan Keluskar,Amrita Bhattacharjee,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多智能体系统中，通过人格提示（如高低宜人性）操纵大语言模型的沟通风格对任务表现的影响。实验覆盖结构化编码、开放式研究协作和竞争性谈判三个领域，发现人格效应高度依赖任务结构：编码任务中沟通风格变化大但影响小，而协作和谈判任务中相同操纵会显著降低性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已证实人格提示能改变LLM的沟通风格（如低宜人性产生对抗性语言，高宜人性产生合作性语言），但沟通风格与任务表现之间的关系尚未在多领域中被系统检验。

Method: 通过操纵前沿LLM的人格特质（宜人性高低），在多智能体团队中设置三个任务域（结构化编码、开放式研究协作、竞争性谈判），系统比较人格组成对团队表现的影响。

Result: 编码任务中低宜人性引起较大沟通风格变化，但对任务完成影响甚微；而在开放式协作和谈判任务中，相同操纵显著损害性能。

Conclusion: 人格对多智能体系统性能的影响关键取决于任务结构，提示工程需根据任务特性调整；同时揭示了人格操纵的局限性。

Abstract: Personality prompting shapes how large language models communicate, yet whether these behavioral shifts affect objective task outcomes remains under-explored. Prior work shows that agents prompted with low agreeableness produce adversarial language, while those prompted with high agreeableness become cooperative, but the relationship between communication style and task performance has not been systematically examined across multiple domains. In this work, we investigate whether personality composition matters for multi-agent team performance by manipulating personality traits across frontier LLMs on three task domains: structured coding, open-ended research collaboration, and competitive bargaining. We find that personality effects depend critically on task structure. In coding tasks, low agreeableness leads to large communication shifts that have little effect on milestone completion. In open-ended collaboration and bargaining, the same manipulation substantially degrades performance. We discuss implications for multi-agent system design and the limits of personality manipulation.

</details>


### [168] [Internalizing the Future: A Unified Agentic Training Paradigm for World Model Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27483)
*Xuan Zhang,Zhijian Zhou,Lingfeng Qiao,Yulei Qin,Ke Li,Xing Sun,Xiaoyu Tan,Chao Qu,Yuan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三阶段训练范式，通过注入世界模型能力、格式化输出和强化学习校准，使LLM智能体在长程任务中具备真正的"what-if"前瞻性推理能力，实验表明该方法显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM智能体在长程任务中本质上是反应式的，缺乏像人类那样通过"what-if"推理评估潜在计划的能力，即缺少用于模拟未来结果的内部世界模型。

Method: 提出三阶段训练范式：(i) World Model Agentic Mid-Training (WM-AMT) 注入潜在预测能力；(ii) Format-Eliciting SFT (FE-SFT) 结构化该能力；(iii) Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (FC-RL) 校准生成模拟的有效性。

Result: 在搜索和数学推理任务中，该方法一致性地优于其他训练基线。

Conclusion: 有效的内部世界建模需要"能力优先"的训练管线，即先注入预测能力、再格式化输出、最后通过强化学习校准，才能实现有根基且校准的前瞻性推理。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have demonstrated strong capability in sequential decision-making, yet they remains fundamentally reactive in long-horizon tasks. Unlike humans who employ "what-if" reasoning to evaluate potential plans before commitment, standard agents lack an internal world model to simulate future outcomes. Therefore, we propose to internalize future-aware planning by training a single autoregressive model to verbalize both a prospective state rollout and a plan-conditioned success estimate-a textual analogue of the Q-value. Crucially, we identify a format-capability gap: simply fine-tuning agents on look-ahead traces during post-training leads to superficial mimicry of foresight without genuine predictive grounding. To bridge this gap, we introduce a three-stage training paradigm: (i) World Model Agentic Mid-Training (WM-AMT) to inject latent predictive capabilities into the policy; (ii) Format-Eliciting SFT (FE-SFT) to structure this injected capability; and (iii) Foresight-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (FC-RL) to refine the calibration and utility of the generated simulations. Evaluated on search and mathematical reasoning tasks, our approach consistently outperforms other training baselines. Our results demonstrate that effective internal world modeling in LLM agents requires a capability-first training pipeline to achieve grounded and calibrated foresight.

</details>


### [169] [Odyssey: Constructing Verifiable Local Truth-Preserving Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27593)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ODYSSEY——一个用于构建可验证、局部保真的基础模型的范畴论框架，将基础模型视为由多种"铸造厂"（foundries）组合而成，每个铸造厂是封装了局部上下文、表示族、限制映射、胶合规则、阻碍策略、更新义务和人类可读视图的知识层。通过左右Kan扩展实现通用铸造厂学习（UFL），并设计了Foundry SQL（FSQL）查询语言和TICKET认证机制。该框架已在多种具体铸造厂上实现和测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基础模型缺乏可验证性、局部真实保持能力以及跨异构源的结构化知识组织方式，需要一种严格的范畴论框架来形式化地构建、组合和验证知识组件。

Method: 以范畴论中的层（sheaf）理论为核心，将知识组织为铸造厂（foundries），每个铸造厂是一个携带论证组件的知识层；通过左Kan扩展将局部构件滚动为候选铸造厂，右Kan扩展强制执行限制、胶合、阻碍和论证条件；采用TICKET（基于因果Kan扩展变换器的拓扑斯集成）进行认证。

Result: 该框架已完整实现并在多种具体铸造厂上测试，支持领域构建、构件回放、层诊断、基于Toulmin/局部LLM的细粒度审查、残留阻碍分类账以及跨异构源的优化因果声明提取。

Conclusion: 同一套范畴论机制能够统一支持多种知识构建和验证任务，为构建可验证、可组合的基础模型提供了严格的理论基础和实践工具。

Abstract: We introduce a categorical framework called ODYSSEY for constructing verifiable, local truth-preserving foundation models as compositions of foundries: building-block architectural components that specify a cover of local contexts, local representation families, restriction maps, gluing rules, obstruction policies, update obligations, and human-facing views. A foundry is an organized sheaf of knowledge that carries within it an argumentation component. Concrete foundries are built from generic foundries such as evidence/argument, operational decision, institutional/financial, market meaning, scientific challenge, research-program, assistant-build, and evaluation-harness foundries. Universal Foundry Learning (UFL) formalizes foundry construction as a composition of left and right Kan extensions, with left Kan extension rolling local artifacts into candidate foundries and right Kan extension enforcing the restriction, gluing, obstruction, and argumentation conditions required for promotion. Foundry SQL (FSQL) is a small typed query surface for slicing maintained foundry artifacts that uses TICKET (Topos Integration using Causal Kan Extension Transformers) certification for admitting external or pre-built models into durable ODYSSEY state. ODYSSEY is fully implemented and tested across a wide spectrum of concrete foundries, showing that the same categorical machinery supports domain construction, artifact replay, sheaf diagnostics, grounded Toulmin/local-LLM scrutiny, residual-obstruction ledgers, and optimized TICKET-compatible causal-claim extraction across heterogeneous sources. This paper is to be presented as a 2.5 hour tutorial at ICML 2026. The tutorial home page is at https://bit.ly/4ajS0nA.

</details>


### [170] [MER-R1: Multimodal Emotion Reasoning via Slow-Fast Thinking Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27652)
*Zhiyuan Han,Beier Zhu,Wenwen Tong,Chengwei Qin,Xinyi Wang,Jiayu Zhang,Jiangnan Chen,Hewei Guo,Dongchuan Ran,Lewei Lu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现显式推理并不一定能提升多模态情感识别（MER）的准确性，尽管它使预测更具可解释性。基于快思（直接回答）和慢思（推理性思考）的互补性，作者提出MER-R1，一种强化学习框架，通过双目标解耦和快慢置信度校准，将快思的召回导向与慢思的精度导向统一起来，实现联合优化，并在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究多模态情感识别中显式推理（慢思）是否真正提升了识别准确性，并解决快思（高召回）与慢思（高精度）之间的权衡问题，使推理切实有益于情感识别。

Method: 提出MER-R1强化学习框架，包含：(1) 双目标解耦，将召回率和精度分离为两个优化信号进行联合优化；(2) 快慢置信度校准，将慢思最终答案与快思直觉对齐，增强正确情感、抑制错误情感；并提供理论证明该协同能减少优化过程中的方差干扰。

Result: 在MER-UniBench和MME-Emotion基准上，MER-R1取得了最先进的性能，验证了快慢思互补协同的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式推理（慢思）虽提升可解释性，但未必提高准确率；快思具有更高召回和置信度，慢思则偏向精确。通过将两者互补性转化为显式优化目标，MER-R1成功使推理真正有益于情感识别任务。

Abstract: We find that explicit reasoning does not necessarily translate into better multimodal emotion recognition (MER) accuracy, even though it makes predictions more interpretable. Specifically, for reasoning-based MLLMs, fast thinking by triggering direct answers often outperforms slow thinking after deliberative reasoning. Our empirical analyses show that fast thinking improves recall with broader and more confident predictions, whereas slow thinking favors precision through conservative filtering of incorrect categories. Building on these insights, we propose MER-R1, a reinforcement learning framework that turns slow-fast complementarity into explicit optimization. Dual-objective disentanglement separates recall and precision into two optimization signals, allowing them to be jointly optimized rather than traded off against each other. Slow-fast confidence calibration further aligns the final slow-thinking answer with fast-thinking intuition, strengthening correct emotions while suppressing incorrect ones. In this way, MER-R1 unifies the recall-oriented intuition of fast thinking with the precision-oriented selectivity of slow thinking. We further provide theoretical justification for this synergy, showing that it mitigates variance-induced interference during optimization. Extensive experiments on MER-UniBench and MME-Emotion show that MER-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance and makes reasoning genuinely benefit emotion recognition.

</details>


### [171] [ToE: A Hierarchical and Explainable Claim Verification Framework with Dynamic Multi-source Evidence Retrieval and Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27736)
*Zhaoqi Wang,Zijian Zhang,Kun Zheng,Zhen Li,Xin Li,Chunlei Li,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tree of Evidence (ToE)框架，通过分层证据推理、强化学习驱动的多源检索代理和论证树聚合算法，实现自动化事实核查，在多个数据集和骨干LLM上取得4-24个百分点的性能提升，尤其对抗性毒化输入效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假新闻的快速传播对信息生态系统构成严重威胁，尤其是生成式引擎优化(GEO)投毒下AI生成的错误信息可通过检索系统被系统性呈现，污染LLM推理过程，亟需可解释的自动化事实核查方法。

Method: ToE框架包含三个核心组件：1) 强化学习驱动的多源检索代理，用于动态收集证据；2) 证据评估代理，对检索到的证据进行可信度评分；3) 论证树聚合算法，将每个主张建模为动态扩展的论证树，通过可解释的证据链迭代分解、检索和验证主张。此外还提供了检索过程的理论误差界分析。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种骨干LLM上，ToE相较竞争基线实现了4到24个百分点的性能提升，其中在对抗性毒化输入上的增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: ToE作为一种层次化证据推理框架，通过将事实核查建模为动态论证树扩展过程，结合强化学习检索策略，有效提升了自动化事实核查的准确性和可解释性，尤其能够抵御GEO投毒攻击下的误导信息。

Abstract: The rapid spread of fake news poses increasing threats to information ecosystems, especially as AI-generated misinformation under Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) poisoning allows adversarially crafted content to be systematically surfaced by retrieval systems, contaminating LLM reasoning. In this paper, we propose Tree of Evidence (ToE), a hierarchical evidence reasoning framework for automated fact-checking that models each claim as a dynamically expanding argument tree. ToE integrates a reinforcement learning-driven multi-source retrieval agent, an evidence evaluation agent, and an argument tree aggregation algorithm to iteratively decompose, retrieve, and verify claims through an explainable evidence chain. We further provide a theoretical analysis of the retrieval process, deriving a formal error bound that guarantees the learned policy converges to a neighborhood of the information-theoretically optimal policy. Experiments across multiple datasets and backbone LLMs demonstrate that ToE achieves improvements ranging from 4 to 24 percentage points over competitive baselines, with particularly pronounced gains on adversarially poisoned inputs.

</details>


### [172] [Towards Reliable and Robust LLM Planning: Symbolic Feedback-Driven Iterative Self-Refinement Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27757)
*Jiajing Zhang,Jiamei Jiang,Chenyang Zhang,Feifei Mo,Linjing Li,Daniel Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种符号反馈驱动的迭代自优化框架，通过自然语言提示机制、符号验证器和计划识别器，提升LLM在长时域规划任务中的鲁棒性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在长时域决策任务中常产生不可行或不正确的规划，存在鲁棒性和可靠性方面的安全隐患。

Method: 1) 自然语言提示机制：将逻辑符号映射为自然语言描述，增强LLM对任务约束和语义的理解；2) 符号验证器：识别错误并转换为LLM可解释的纠正指令，引导自我改进；3) 计划识别器：推断目标可达性，更有效地引导规划向期望目标前进。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架在长时域规划任务中持续提升了规划的可行性和正确性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效增强了基于LLM的规划可靠性，有望构建更可信的AI系统。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have attracted widespread attention from academia and industry, yet their deployment raises critical security concerns regarding robustness and reliability. Planning, a core component of intelligent behavior, remains challenging for LLMs, which often produce infeasible or incorrect solutions in long-horizon decision-making tasks due to inherent complexity. In this paper, we propose a symbolic feedback-driven iterative self-refinement framework to enhance the robustness and reliability of LLMs in long-horizon planning. Specifically, a natural language prompting mechanism is introduced to map logical symbols into natural language descriptions, enabling LLMs to better capture task constraints and semantics. We further design a symbolic verifier that identifies errors and converts them into corrective instructions interpretable by the LLM, thereby guiding self-refinement. In addition, we leverage a plan recognizer to infer goal reachability, facilitating more effective guidance toward desired goals. Empirical results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves both feasibility and correctness in long-horizon planning tasks. This highlights its effectiveness in enhancing the reliability of LLM-based planning and potential to enable more trustworthy AI systems.

</details>


### [173] [Understanding Rollout Error in Graph World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27780)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了图世界模型（GWM）中的长程 rollout 误差，提出了统一框架和误差界，并设计了一种误差感知的 GWM 方法（结合谱正则化、rollout 一致性和关键节点加权），在合成拓扑和真实图基准上验证了该方法能防止长程发散并提升规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多规划环境本质上是图结构（如智能体、工具、技能、路线和依赖关系图），而非传统的向量或图像。在这些场景中，局部预测误差可能通过图传播，且当边是动态预测而非固定时，失败模式会发生变化。现有研究缺乏对这种图世界模型中长程 rollout 误差的系统分析。

Method: 提出了统一的固定边和动态边 GWM 框架，引入动作节点以支持节点级、边级和图级决策；推导了图值 rollout 误差界，将拓扑引起的放大与模型引起的放大分离；引入联合节点-边算子用于动态边 rollout；基于理论分析设计了 Error-Aware GWM，包含谱正则化、rollout 一致性和关键节点加权。

Result: 在合成拓扑和异构智能体-图测试平台中，rollout 误差和规划遗憾随 horizon 增长而增大；当结构演化时需要动态边训练；Error-Aware GWM 能防止长程发散同时保持预测精度。真实图基准表明 GWM 在动态图 rollout 和智能体规划中最有用，而专用图模型在静态或稀疏预测任务上仍具优势。

Conclusion: 图世界模型适用于动态图场景下的规划，Error-Aware GWM 有效缓解了长程 rollout 误差发散问题，但静态或稀疏预测任务仍应使用专用图模型。

Abstract: World models are often used for planning by rolling learned dynamics forward. Many planning environments, however, are not vectors or images; they are graphs of agents, tools, skills, routes, and dependencies. In these settings, a local prediction error may stay local or spread through the graph, and the failure mode changes again when edges are predicted rather than fixed. This paper studies long-horizon rollout error in Graph World Models (GWMs). We formulate a unified fixed-edge and dynamic-edge GWM framework with action nodes for node-, edge-, and graph-level decisions. We develop graph-valued rollout bounds that separate topology-induced amplification from model-induced amplification, and we introduce a joint node-edge operator for dynamic-edge rollouts. Guided by the analysis, we propose Error-Aware GWM, which combines spectral regularization, rollout consistency, and critical-node weighting. Across synthetic topologies and heterogeneous agent-graph testbeds, rollout error and planning regret grow with horizon, dynamic-edge training is needed when structure evolves, and Error-Aware GWM prevents long-horizon divergence while preserving prediction accuracy. Real-world graph benchmarks clarify the scope of GWMs: they are most useful for dynamic graph rollout and agent planning, while specialized graph models remain strong on static or sparse prediction tasks.

</details>


### [174] [Grounded Iterative Language Planning: How Parameterized World Models Reduce Hallucination Propagation in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27806)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种用于语言智能体的世界模型（基于智能体的世界模型和参数化世界模型），并提出Grounded Iterative Language Planning（GILP）方法，通过训练小型参数化骨干网络并结合API-based智能体推理，在四个图结构规划基准上显著降低了幻觉状态率（从0.176降至0.035），并将成功率从0.668提升至0.838，仅增加约22%的额外LLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两种世界模型各有优劣：基于智能体的世界模型（调用LLM API）灵活但易产生幻觉状态变化且难以用回归损失衡量；参数化世界模型（训练过渡预测器）误差易量化但作为独立规划器能力较弱。需要一种方法结合两者优势。

Method: 提出GILP（Grounded Iterative Language Planning），训练小型参数化骨干网络，提供有效动作、预测状态增量、风险和价值；LLM负责草拟动作和想象增量；通过一致性门控机制，当两者不一致时要求修订。

Result: 在真实GPT-4o-mini调用中，幻觉状态率从0.176降至0.035；在校准模拟器消融实验中，成功率从0.668提升至0.838，仅增加约22%的额外LLM调用。

Conclusion: GILP通过结合参数化模型的可量化误差和基于智能体模型的灵活推理，有效减少了幻觉状态，提升了规划成功率，且额外计算成本可控。

Abstract: World models for language agents come in two useful forms. An agent-based world model calls an LLM API and reasons flexibly in language, but its errors appear as hallucinated state changes that are hard to score with ordinary regression losses. A parameterized world model is a trained transition predictor; its errors are easier to measure with quantities such as NodeMSE, delta accuracy, and validity accuracy, but it is usually weaker as a standalone planner. We compare these two families on four graph-structured planning benchmarks and introduce operational hallucination metrics for the agent-based case. The comparison motivates \textbf{Grounded Iterative Language Planning} (GILP), which trains only a small parameterized backbone and combines it with API-based agent reasoning. The backbone supplies valid actions, predicted state deltas, risk, and value; the LLM drafts an action and imagined delta; and a consistency gate asks for revision when the two disagree. On real GPT-4o-mini calls, GILP reduces hallucinated-state rate from 0.176 to 0.035. In calibrated simulator ablations, it raises success from 0.668 to 0.838 while adding only ~22% extra LLM calls.

</details>


### [175] [ATOD: Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation for Multi-turn Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27814)
*Qitai Tan,Zefang Zong,Yang Li,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ATOD是一种混合在线蒸馏算法，通过退火式OPD-RL调度和回合级不确定性重加权，在长周期交互任务中同时实现快速模仿学习和基于奖励的探索，在多个基准上超越教师模型和现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD在早期训练效率高但性能上限受限于教师水平，RL能突破上限但早期学习效率低。作者旨在利用两者的互补性，设计一种混合方法同时实现高效早期学习和更高最终性能。

Method: (1) 退火式OPD-RL调度：早期以OPD为主快速接近教师行为，逐步增强RL以驱动基于奖励的探索。(2) Turn-level Disagreement-Uncertainty Reweighting (T-DUR)：在长轨迹中软性放大高价值回合，改善密集监督。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA上，ATOD在三个学生规模下平均成功率比OPD提升3.03个百分点，比GRPO提升23.62个百分点，并超越对应教师模型2.16个百分点。

Conclusion: ATOD有效融合了OPD和RL的互补优势，通过退火调度和回合级重加权机制，在长周期交互任务中实现了超越教师模型的性能。

Abstract: Training small language-model agents for long-horizon interactive tasks requires both fast imitation and reward-driven improvement. On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher guidance and typically improves rapidly in the early stage, but its gains saturate once the student approaches the teacher, limiting the final performance ceiling. Reinforcement learning (RL) directly optimizes environment rewards and encourages exploratory improvement toward a higher reward-defined ceiling, but sparse and delayed feedback makes early-stage learning much less efficient than OPD. In this paper, we propose ATOD (Annealed Turn-aware On-policy Distillation), a hybrid online distillation algorithm that explicitly exploits this complementarity. (1) ATOD uses an annealed OPD-RL schedule: OPD dominates early training to approach teacher-level behavior, while RL is gradually strengthened to drive reward-based exploration. (2) ATOD introduces Turn-level Disagreement-Uncertainty Reweighting (T-DUR), which softly amplifies high-utility turns and improves dense supervision in long trajectories. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA show that ATOD consistently outperforms competing post-training baselines: across the three student sizes, ATOD improves average success rate by 3.03 points over OPD and 23.62 points over GRPO, while surpassing the corresponding teacher models by 2.16 points.

</details>


### [176] [NormAct: A Benchmark for Hidden Social Norm Compliance in Embodied Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27826)
*Shiyun Zhao,Xinwei Song,Tianyu Guo,Xiaomeng Gao,Mingyuan Liu,Xu Han,Yuanyuan Zhang,Zhenliang Zhang,Xue Feng,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NormAct基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在具身规划中遵守隐性社会规范的能力。实验发现SOTA模型在显式目标达成（67.3%）与隐式规范遵守（26.4%）之间存在显著差距，并提出NormPerceptor方法将任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在具身环境中的规划能力时，通常只关注显式目标达成或直接规范知识，而忽略了模型推断并应用隐性社会规范的能力。本文旨在填补这一评估空白。

Method: 构建NormAct基准，将隐性社会规范嵌入日常任务中，从目标达成、规范遵守和总体任务成功三个维度评估。提出NormPerceptor，一种上下文条件化线索生成器，在规划前推断场景相关规范。

Result: GPT-5.4、Claude Opus 4.7、Gemini 3 Pro等SOTA模型在显式目标上达成率67.3%，但隐性规范遵守率仅26.4%。NormPerceptor将任务成功率从24.2%提升至46.7%。

Conclusion: MLLMs并非缺乏社会知识，而是难以在上下文中激活和落地相关规范。需要赋予具身代理主动检测隐性规范、将其在视觉证据中落地并整合为规划约束的能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed as embodied planners in egocentric environments, where task success requires not only achieving instructed goals but also acting in socially appropriate ways. While explicit goals may render certain actions optimal, implicit social norms often impose hidden constraints. Existing evaluations typically focus on explicit goal achievement or direct norm knowledge, seldom assessing whether planners can infer and apply these hidden constraints within action sequences. We introduce NormAct, a benchmark for embodied social-norm interactions that evaluates plans on Goal Achievement, Norm Compliance, and overall Task Success. NormAct uniquely embeds hidden norms within ordinary tasks, testing whether models can realize them without explicit instruction. Experiments with state-of-the-art MLLMs (GPT-5.4, Claude Opus 4.7, Gemini 3 Pro) reveal a significant gap: models achieve explicit goals in 67.3\% of cases, but comply with hidden norms in only 26.4\%. Cue-condition experiments indicate that this gap stems not from a lack of general social knowledge, but from challenges in activating and grounding relevant norms in context. To address this, we propose NormPerceptor, a context-conditioned cue generator that infers scene-relevant norms prior to planning, increasing Task Success from 24.2\% to 46.7\%. Our results underscore the importance of enabling embodied agents to proactively detect hidden norms, ground them in visual evidence, and integrate them as action-planning constraints. Our benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Caleb196x/NormAct.

</details>


### [177] [Verifiable Geometry Problem Solving: Solver-Driven Autoformalization and Theorem Proposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27926)
*Can Li,Ting Zhang,Junbo Zhao,Hua Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SD-GPS 提出了一种求解器驱动的几何问题求解框架，通过将符号求解器作为执行预言机，在自动形式化阶段引入可执行性监督学习，在定理预测阶段引入卡点感知的辅助引理生成机制，显著提升了神经符号几何推理的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号几何求解框架存在两个核心瓶颈：一是自动形式化将多模态翻译视为静态任务，与下游求解器兼容性脱节；二是定理预测阶段因固定规则库而频繁陷入演绎困境（无法继续推理）。

Method: 提出 SD-GPS 框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 求解器驱动的自动形式化——基于 QwenVL3-2B 构建统一模块，融合监督形式语言适应与可求解性引导的强化学习，以可执行性为核心训练信号；(2) 验证的定理提出——引入卡点感知的智能体，从当前证明状态中提出局部辅助引理，并通过符号验证过滤所有提议以确保可靠性。

Result: 在 Geometry3K 和 PGPS9K 基准上，SD-GPS 在标准补全、多项选择和跨模态参照任务中一致优于现有的多模态大模型、纯神经网络和神经符号方法。

Conclusion: 多模态感知与符号执行之间的闭环能显著提升几何推理能力，为神经智能体如何通过形式化系统落地以实现可验证的问题求解能力提供了深刻洞见。

Abstract: Geometry Problem Solving have increasingly adopt the neuro-symbolic paradigm, combining neural intuition with symbolic rigor. However, current frameworks suffer from severe bottlenecks in two core stages: autoformalization, which treats multimodal translation as a static task decoupled from downstream solver compatibility, and theorem prediction, where solvers frequently hit a deductive impasse due to fixed rule libraries. To address these, we propose SD-GPS, a solver-driven framework that treats the symbolic solver as an execution oracle throughout both formalization and deduction. First, Solver-Driven Autoformalization unifies supervised formal-language adaptation and solvability-guided reinforcement learning into a single module built on QwenVL3-2B, making executability the central training signal. Second, Verified Theorem Proposing introduces an impasse-aware agent that proposes local auxiliary lemmas from current proof states, ensuring soundness by filtering all proposals through symbolic verification. Empirical evaluations on Geometry3K and PGPS9K demonstrate that SD-GPS consistently outperforms existing MLLM, neural, and neuro-symbolic methods across standard completion, multiple-choice, and cross-modal reference regimes, proving that closing the loop between multimodal perception and symbolic execution significantly improves geometric reasoning, offering profound insights into how neural agents can be grounded by formal systems to achieve verifiable problem-solving capabilities.

</details>


### [178] [RelBall: Relation Ball with Quaternion Rotation for Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.27967)
*Yike Liu,Peijia Xie,Chao He,Huiling Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RelBall是一种扩展Rotate3D的知识图谱补全方法，通过模变换建模层次结构、通过尾实体关系球建模多种基数关系，能够覆盖对称、反对称、逆、组合及语义层次等所有关系模式，并在多个数据集上取得竞争性链接预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGC模型如RotatE能捕捉对称、反对称、逆和交换组合模式，但无法处理非交换组合；Rotate3D引入非交换性但无法建模语义层次结构，且两者均不能有效处理一对多关系。

Method: RelBall在Rotate3D基础上引入两项创新：(1) 模变换（Modulus Transformation）通过调整模长将抽象概念映射到较小模、具体实例映射到较大模，从而建模层次结构；(2) 尾实体关系球（Tail-centric Relation Ball）用于建模一对一、一对多、多对一和多对多关系。

Result: 在多个数据集上的链接预测实验表明，RelBall相比各类基线方法取得了竞争性的性能表现。

Conclusion: RelBall具备三大优势：覆盖所有关系模式；提供可解释的层次化表示，模长直接反映语义层级；支持多种基数关系类型。

Abstract: Real-world knowledge graphs are often incomplete, lacking many valid facts. Knowledge Graph Completion (KGC) aims to predict missing links using known triples, thereby enhancing graph coverage. A key challenge is modeling diverse relational patterns such as symmetry, antisymmetry, inversion, composition and semantic hierarchy. Existing models such as RotatE can capture symmetric, antisymmetric, inverse, and commutative composition patterns, yet struggle with non-commutative composition. Rotate3D addresses this by introducing non-commutativity via three-dimensional rotations, but still fails to capture the semantic hierarchies prevalent in knowledge graphs. Moreover, both models cannot effectively model one-to-many relations. To overcome these limitations, we propose RelBall, which extends Rotate3D with two innovations. First, our model introduces modulus transformation to model hierarchies, driving abstract concepts toward smaller moduli and concrete instances toward larger ones. Second, it introduces a tail-centric relation ball to model one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many relations. RelBall offers the following advantages: (1) coverage of all relational patterns, including the ones mentioned above; (2) an interpretable hierarchical representation where the modulus directly reflect semantic levels; (3) support for one-to-one, one-to-many, many-to-one, and many-to-many relations. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate RelBall's competitive link prediction performance against various baselines.

</details>


### [179] [Lifted Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28024)
*Malte Luttermann,Tanya Braun,Ralf Möller,Marcel Gehrke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出参数化因果因子图（PCFGs）及其部分有向变体（PD-PCFGs），并在此基础上设计提升因果推断（LCI）算法，实现在关系域中以提升方式高效计算因果效应，显著加速因果推断过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果推断方法多在命题层面进行，计算复杂度高；而提升推理技术已在概率图模型中成功用于加速查询回答，但尚未被系统地应用于因果推断。本文旨在将提升技术引入因果推断，以在关系域中高效计算因果效应。

Method: 1) 提出参数化因果因子图（PCFG），将因果知识融入提升模型，并给出干预的形式化语义；2) 设计提升因果推断（LCI）算法，在提升层面计算因果效应；3) 提出部分有向参数化因果因子图（PD-PCFG），处理部分因果知识，并扩展LCI算法以适用于更广泛的模型。

Result: LCI算法在提升层面进行因果推断，相比命题层面的因果贝叶斯网络等方法，大幅加速了因果效应的计算。PD-PCFG的引入进一步扩展了提升因果推断的适用范围，降低了对因果关系的先验知识要求。

Conclusion: 提升技术可有效应用于因果推断，PCFG和LCI为关系域中的高效因果效应计算提供了理论基础和实用算法；PD-PCFG提升了方法的灵活性和适用性。

Abstract: Lifted inference exploits indistinguishabilities in probabilistic graphical models by using a representative for indistinguishable objects, thereby speeding up query answering while maintaining exact answers. In this article, we show how lifting can be applied to efficiently compute causal effects in relational domains. More specifically, we introduce parametric causal factor graphs (PCFGs) to incorporate causal knowledge in lifted models and give a formal semantics of interventions therein. We further present the Lifted Causal Inference (LCI) algorithm to compute causal effects on a lifted level, thereby drastically speeding up causal inference compared to propositional inference, e.g., in causal Bayesian networks. In addition, we present partially directed parametric causal factor graphs (PD-PCFGs) as a generalisation of PCFGs to handle partial causal knowledge and extend LCI to perform lifted causal inference in a PD-PCFG, thereby extending the applicability of lifted causal inference to a broader range of models requiring less prior knowledge about causal relationships.

</details>


### [180] [JD Oxygen AI Item Center (Oxygen AIIC) V1: An Industrial-Scale LLM/VLM-Centric Solution for Item Understanding, Management, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28070)
*Oxygen AIIC,Chan Long,Chao Liu,Chaofan Chen,Chaohui Dong,Chunyuan Guo,Danping Liu,Debin Liu,Deping Xiang,Fulai Xu,Guangyue Liu,Hao Li,Huichun Hu,Jian Yang,Jianan Wang,Jianbo Zhao,Jiaoyang Li,Jiaxing Wang,Jinglong Li,Jinjin Guo,Jun Fang,Jun Liu,Kai Zhou,Li Wang,Lili Gao,Liying Chen,Luning Yang,Mengdi Zhou,Pengzhang Liu,Qi Lv,Qianyun Wang,Qixia Jiang,Ruyue Li,Shimu Liang,Shuxing Wang,Sijie Zhang,Siqi Li,Tianhao Gao,Wang Ke,Weihu Huang,Wencan Lai,Wenjie Zhang,Xiaohui Zhang,Xiaojing Dong,Ya Liu,Yifeng Zhang,Yixiang Wang,Yongtai Zhang,Yongyi Liao,Zhaoru Chen,Zhen Chen,Zhiyong Ma,Zhiyuan Liu,Zhongwei Liu,Ziyan Xing*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 京东推出基于LLM/VLM的工业级商品知识生产与服务平台Oxygen AIIC，覆盖数十亿SKU，实现94.2%精确率和82.8%召回率的知识生产，日均处理数亿次商品更新，搜索流量覆盖率达80.4%，核心属性自动化填充率超80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 京东作为服务7亿+用户、数十亿SKU的电商平台，面临三大工业级挑战：快速涌现的新概念、海量SKU的高质量知识生产、多样化的下游业务需求，需要构建大规模、高质量的结构化商品知识体系。

Method: 提出四大核心支柱：(i) 高效人机协同驱动的本体工程，支持百万级本体动态演化；(ii) "语义搜索后判别"(S2D)知识识别架构配合吞吐优化策略，实现可扩展、高吞吐的AI商品库生产；(iii) 自我进化的商品理解LLM/VLM，稳定可控地提升性能；(iv) 统一商品通道作为数据与服务枢纽。

Result: 知识生产精确率94.2%、召回率82.8%；覆盖数万京东类目；日均处理数亿次商品更新（基于华为昇腾NPU）；累计数百亿商品知识资产；搜索流量覆盖率80.4%；商品信息质量问题下降37%；商品上架核心属性自动填充率超80%。

Conclusion: Oxygen AIIC作为工业级平台，成功解决了超大规模电商场景下的商品知识生产与服务挑战，在搜索、推荐、运营、类目规划等核心业务场景中实现了可量化的规模级收益。

Abstract: JD.com, one of the world's largest e-commerce platforms, serves over 700 million active users and millions of merchants, with a catalog of tens of billions of SKUs. At this scale, high-quality, structured item knowledge underpins a better consumer experience, lower management costs, and higher operational efficiency-yet producing and serving it poses three industrial-scale challenges: fast-emerging concepts, high-quality knowledge production for massive SKUs, and diverse downstream requirements. To address these challenges, we present the JD Oxygen AI Item Center (Oxygen AIIC), an industrial-scale platform built on LLMs/VLMs for item-knowledge production and service. Oxygen AIIC is built around four core pillars: (i) ontology engineering driven by efficient human-AI collaboration, which supports the dynamic evolution and agile expansion of an ontology with millions of entries; (ii) a "Semantic Search then Discrimination"(S2D) knowledge identification architecture that, combined with throughput improvement strategies, enables scalable, extensible, and high-throughput AI Item Library production for tens of billions of SKUs; (iii) self-evolving item-understanding LLMs/VLMs that improve in a stable and controllable manner, enabling knowledge production with 94.2% precision and 82.8% recall; and (iv) a unified item tunnel that serves as the data and service hub. Oxygen AIIC now covers tens of thousands of JD categories and processes hundreds of millions of item updates per day on Huawei Ascend NPUs. It has accumulated hundreds of billions of item-knowledge assets. Deployed across core business scenarios-including search, recommendation, operations, category planning-Oxygen AIIC has delivered measurable gains at scale. Search-traffic coverage reaches 80.4%, item-information quality issues drop by 37%, the automated fill rate of core attributes during item listing exceeds 80%.

</details>


### [181] [Ontology-Guided Evidence Path Inference for Multi-hop Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28076)
*Yongxue Shan,Meihan Wu,Cundi Fang,Jie Peng,Xiaodong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OPI框架，通过本体引导的路径推理解决多跳KGQA中的搜索空间膨胀和语义约束问题，在WebQSP、CWQ和MetaQA上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于主题中心扩展的多跳KGQA方法面临两大挑战：搜索空间随噪声混合类型路径快速增长，且检索路径可能无法满足复杂问题的语义约束。

Method: 提出OPI框架：1）构建关系中心本体图，捕获关系的头尾类型约束；2）双向检索机制，通过预测答案类型匹配兼容最终跳关系，结合主题侧前缀扩展与答案侧最终跳匹配；3）迭代精炼策略，在问题上下文下重新评估检索路径和候选答案。

Result: 在WebQSP上Hit@1/F1提升4.6/5.0点，在CWQ上提升8.9/3.3点，在MetaQA上仅用检索模块即达到接近饱和的Hit@1。

Conclusion: OPI通过本体引导的路径推理有效抑制噪声扩展、满足语义约束，显著提升多跳KGQA性能并减小搜索空间。

Abstract: Knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) aims to answer natural-language questions by reasoning over structured facts. Existing multi-hop KGQA methods mainly rely on topic-centered expansion, which faces two key challenges: the search space rapidly grows with noisy mixed-type paths, and retrieved paths may fail to satisfy the semantic constraints of complex questions. To address these challenges, we propose OPI, an ontology-guided evidence path inference framework for multi-hop KGQA. OPI introduces a relation-centric ontology graph to capture the head-tail type constraints of relations, providing a compact interface for answer-side constraints. Based on this ontology graph, OPI first introduces a bidirectional retrieval mechanism by mapping the predicted answer type to compatible final-hop relations and combining topic-side prefix expansion with answer-side final-hop matching, thereby suppressing noisy mixed-type expansion. OPI further adopts an iterative refinement strategy to reassess retrieved paths and candidate answers under the question context, filtering type-compatible but question-irrelevant evidence for more reliable answer prediction. Experiments on WebQSP, CWQ, and MetaQA show that OPI substantially reduces the search space, improves Hit@1/F1 by 4.6/5.0 points on WebQSP and 8.9/3.3 points on CWQ over the strongest prior results, and achieves near-saturated Hit@1 on MetaQA with the retrieval module alone.

</details>


### [182] [AI-Driven Synthesis for High-Tech System Design: Automating Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28126)
